about work anymore.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S024 (Depressed_Mood): Most days lately I just sit in my place feeling this heavy, empty kind of sadness, like there’s no point to anything and I could start crying over nothing if I let myself think about it too long.\n- S025 (Loss_of_Interest): I used to look forward to walking by the lake or messing with my model cars, but now even on my days off I just lie on the couch staring at the TV without really watching it, not caring about anything or anyone calling me.\n- S026 (Weight_Appetite_Change): Since all this started, I barely feel like eating, and I have to force down a sandwich once a day while my pants are getting loose because food just doesn’t appeal to me anymore.\n- S027 (Sleep_Disturbance): Most nights I toss lifting.\n- S030 (Worthlessness_Guilt): I keep telling myself I’m a screwup, that losing hours at work is all my fault because I’m lazy and useless, and I replay every little mistake I ever made like proof that I don’t deserve any better.\n- S031 (Diminished_Thinking): When I try to sit down and figure out what bills to pay or even just read a simple form, my brain feels like it’s full of fog and guys at work, telling my cousin I could build up my own handyman business easy and make more money than my old boss without even really thinking it through.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): Back when I was feeling wired about those new work ideas, I’d stay up almost all night watching videos about starting a business and sketching out jobs, sleep maybe three hours, and still wake up feeling like I didn’t need any more rest.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): In that same period I couldn’t shut up, talking my cousin’s ear off for an hour straight about all my plans and bouncing from one idea to the next so fast she couldn’t even get a word in.\n- S038 (Racing_Thoughts): My thoughts were racing so fast then that I’d jump from fixing sinks to advertising to buying a truck in my head in just a few seconds, and I couldn’t slow it down or focus on one thing long enough to really figure it out.\n- S040 (Increased_Goal_Activity): I went from barely getting by at my regular job to suddenly making long lists of side gigs, messaging old customers, and trying to schedule a bunch of repairs at once, even though I didn’t really have the tools or time to follow through on half of it.\n▸ Additional about work anymore.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S024 (Depressed_Mood): Most days lately I just sit in my place feeling this heavy, empty kind of sadness, like there’s no point to anything and I could start crying over nothing if I let myself think about it too long.\n- S025 (Loss_of_Interest): I used to look forward to walking by the lake or messing with my model cars, but now even on my days off I just lie on the couch staring at the TV without really watching it, not caring about anything or anyone calling me.\n- S026 (Weight_Appetite_Change): Since all this started, I barely feel like eating, and I have to force down a sandwich once a day while my pants are getting loose because food just doesn’t appeal to me anymore.\n- S027 (Sleep_Disturbance): Most nights I toss and turn for hours, then wake up at three or four with my mind stuck on the rent and the bills and can’t fall back asleep, so I just lie there exhausted until the sun comes up.\n- S028 (Psychomotor_Changes): I move like I’m underwater now, just shuffling around the apartment and taking forever to get dressed or wash the dishes because even lifting my arms feels like too much work.\n- S029 (Fatigue): No matter how long I lie in bed or on the couch, my body feels drained, like just walking to the corner store or up the stairs wears me out like I did a full day of heavy lifting.\n- S030 (Worthlessness_Guilt): I keep telling myself I’m a screwup, that losing hours at work is all my fault because I’m lazy and useless, and I replay every little mistake I ever made like proof that I don’t deserve any better.\n- S031 (Diminished_Thinking): When I try to sit down and figure out what bills to pay or even just read a simple form, my brain feels like it’s full of fog and I lose track halfway through, going over the same line again and again without anything sticking.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): Right after they cut my hours I had this weird stretch where I felt almost too good, snapping at people if they questioned me but also acting like nothing could stop me, like I was on top of the world and everyone else was moving too slow.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): During that high spell I was pacing around my apartment nonstop, cleaning, rearranging tools, writing out big plans on scrap paper and calling people about jobs all at once because I felt like I had to keep moving and doing something every minute.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): For a few days there I really believed I was smarter and more talented than all the other guys at work, telling my cousin I could build up my own handyman business easy and make more money than my old boss without even really thinking it through.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): Back when I was feeling wired about those new work ideas, I’d stay up almost all night watching videos about starting a business and sketching out jobs, sleep maybe three hours, and still wake up feeling like I didn’t need any more rest.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): In that same period I couldn’t shut up, talking my cousin’s ear off for an hour straight about all my plans and bouncing from one idea to the next so fast she couldn’t even get a word in.\n- S038 (Racing_Thoughts): My thoughts were racing so fast then that I’d jump from fixing sinks to advertising to buying a truck in my head in just a few seconds, and I couldn’t slow it down or focus on one thing long enough to really figure it out.\n- S040 (Increased_Goal_Activity): I went from barely getting by at my regular job to suddenly making long lists of side gigs, messaging old customers, and trying to schedule a bunch of repairs at once, even though I didn’t really have the tools or time to follow through on half of it.\n▸ Additional
